description: Since 1985, Papa Rocco's has been serving up award-winning pizza, fresh Gulf oysters, baked wings and unforgettable memories in the heart of Gulf Shores, Alabama. Founded by local legend Bill "Papa" McGinnes, our restaurant quickly became known as the "Home of Warm Beer and Lousy Pizza"—a tongue-in-cheek motto that reflects our fun-loving spirit and coastal charm. More than just a place to eat, Papa Rocco's is a community gathering spot where locals and visitors come together for great food, live music, and good times. From our famous Who's Your Daddy Burger to all-you-can-eat spaghetti nights, we've got something for everyone, all just steps from the beach. Today, Papa Rocco's continues to carry forward Papa's legacy of hospitality and humor. Whether you're here for oysters, pizza, wings, or a night of live music, you're part of the family at Papa Rocco's—where the food is always fresh, the atmosphere is always lively, and the memories last long after the meal.

Fortunately, this is our last night in Alabama.

reviewer_name: Robin Settle
rating: 5
body: This was a fun place! It is basically a sports bar that serves great food. I had the manicotti with Alfredo sauce and my husband had the All Meat pizza.  Both were excellent, as was our appetizer, the stuffed mushrooms.  Though they were technically stuffed, they were really more like 'filled' (think gooey pizza topping - really good!).

The atmosphere was relaxed, the service was wonderful, and they even had live entertainment that night.

As we were leaving, we saw the sign to 'Feed the Fish' for 50 cents in their pond out back.  How cool! Definitely taking some quarters next time, because we will be back.
